Cadence/trigger | Required review
Before publication/campaign | Claims, prices, links, locale, consent, tracking goal, real test lead, staff capacity and budget approval.
Daily during active operations | Unassigned leads/chats, failed delivery, destination errors, account/feed health, alerts and suspicious changes.
Weekly | Campaign quality/spend, search/content backlog, experiment guardrails, automation failures, broken links and reporting freshness.
Monthly | Permissions, authentication/delivery, dependency/security updates, retention/rights tasks, usage/cost and owner usability.
Quarterly or risk-based | Restore/portability exercise, incident rehearsal, provider/API/policy review, accessibility/performance regression and legal applicability.
Annual editorial review | Review articles, ownership, claims and relevance; create human tasks only, never automatic blog deletion.
Urgent change | Exploited vulnerability, privacy incident, provider deprecation, restricted account, law/policy change or business-model change triggers immediate triage.
Observation | Investigation order | Controlled response
No or low delivery | Policy, billing, dates, account access, eligible inventory, targeting conflicts, bids/budget, goal restrictions | Fix the identified cause; do not blindly raise spend.
Low visibility with acceptable economics | Relevant opportunity, available impression-share/lost-share evidence, auction context, bid limit, creative/landing relevance | Test a bounded change with a cost guardrail.
Clicks but few landing sessions | Redirect chain, consent/measurement coverage, load failure, in-app browser, accidental destination | Separate lost measurement from actual failed visits.
Leads but poor qualification | Offer clarity, search intent, spam, geography, form expectation, staff response | Improve targeting/offer or qualification feedback, not fake values.
Reported conversions exceed business outcomes | Duplicate sources, goal configuration, counts, imported events, test data, attribution windows | Reconcile identities and settings before using the figures.
Sudden apparent performance decline | Freshness, outages, conversion lag, account changes, seasonality, real funnel behavior | Annotate the cause and use a reviewed repair or experiment.
Diagnostic dimension | Required owner interpretation
Delivery and reach | Placement-specific exposure and aggregate reach; not a named visitor list.
Creative engagement | Video, click and message milestones with their actual definitions; not sales.
Destination completion | Page availability, successful form/native-lead retrieval, in-app browser and support workflow.
Matching and deduplication | Safe same-action IDs, field eligibility, current diagnostics and final serialized request; not permission to collect more.
Lead quality | Mature accepted/qualified/won cohorts, spam reasons, staff response and realized value.
Audience/creative saturation | Review available frequency, overlap, complaints and cost trends against a declared analysis period.
Account health | Restrictions, rejected assets/events, permissions, billing, API changes and unresolved review tasks.
Route condition | Required default decision | Verification
Published canonical content | Success status, coherent self-canonical, crawlable links and eligible sitemap entry | Raw response plus rendered fixture.
Permanent equivalent move | Intentional permanent redirect to a relevant equivalent, preserving permitted campaign data | No loops, open redirects or unrelated target.
Temporary A/B split | Temporary test routing and declared canonical treatment | Same eligible audience, safe control and test removal.
Removed without relevant replacement | Deliberate 404/410 with helpful navigation | No generic redirect to home; source retention unaffected.
Search/filter result | Explicit finite indexing/crawling policy and accessible navigation | Empty/out-of-range/duplicate parameters handled.
Protected or draft resource | Authentication and no public leakage; appropriate indexing/cache restrictions | Direct URL, download, preview and API tests.
Temporary infrastructure failure | Honest retryable server state; appropriate retry guidance | No mass permanent redirects or blanket content deletion.
Retired product/job/event | Business-specific availability and public retention decision | Feed/markup/UI agree; archived source remains.
Channel family | Required operating package | Meaningful measurement
Organic search and local | Intent/topic map, useful pages, verified profiles and editorial review | Qualified organic demand and customer tasks, not ranking alone.
Paid search/social/video/display | Approved campaign, creative, spend and destination contracts | Mature qualified outcomes with stated attribution.
Owned email/messaging/push | Explicit preferences, templates, suppression, delivery and support | Delivered/answered/converted milestones where observable.
Social publishing and community | Account rights, content calendar, accessible media, moderation and escalation | Referral and useful engagement without invented person-level attribution.
Partnerships/affiliates/referrals | Agreements, disclosures, source codes, reward rules and fraud/reversal controls | Verified referred outcomes and net cost.
PR, events and offline | Authorized spokesperson/assets, registration, QR/source codes, follow-up purpose | Declared/observed acquisition kept distinct.
Retention and advocacy | Onboarding, service milestones, feedback, renewal, cancellation and ethical review requests | Retention cohorts, satisfaction and realized repeat business.
Threat or failure | Required drill
Privilege and tenant boundary | Change IDs/site headers, reuse expired tokens, query exports and jobs, and verify object authorization everywhere.
Upload/URL abuse | Test disguised files, oversized archives, active documents, unsafe redirects and outbound requests toward internal services.
Session and recovery | Revoke a session in another tab; replay a used recovery link; test logout followed by browser restoration.
Provider compromise | Revoke credentials, reject forged webhooks, restrict egress and prevent secret-bearing retries/logs.
Source-content deletion | Attempt DELETE/TRUNCATE, cascades, cleanup jobs, storage lifecycle expiry and unauthorized purge execution.
Audit loss/tampering | Fail local audit persistence, interrupt export, alter manifests and verify protected actions and alerts.
Financial integrity | Replay/out-of-order payment callbacks, mismatched amounts/currency, partial refund and duplicate fulfilment.
Abuse and cost | Stress forms, OTP/messages, geo and AI endpoints with budgets and graceful legitimate-user fallback.

Marketplace/directory | Seller/listing verification, ownership claims, offers, commissions, provider-led split payments, disputes, reviews, fraud and suspension.

Publisher advertising | Inventory/provider configuration, consent, layout/performance budgets, disclosures, eligibility, fraud/viewability reporting and reconciliation.

Gate | Required proof before proceeding
Scope and authority | Approved site brief, activated modules, genuine offers, account ownership, budget and policy decisions.
Public destination | Real HTTPS routes, mobile/in-app/keyboard usability, correct prices/terms, working contact and no accidental crawl blocks.
Business capture | Form/chat/native lead safely accepted, assigned and acknowledged; duplicate/error path tested.
Measurement | Effective goals, consent and final payload policy, consistent event identities, diagnostics and clear data limitations.

Controlled campaign activation | Exact approved remote configuration, bounded initial spend and staffed response workflow.
Scaling | Mature outcome quality, acceptable economics, stable conversion feedback and sufficient service capacity.

Existing CMS, CRM, ad/analytics accounts, and data sources | To be completed.
Data categories, purposes, jurisdictions, and legal reviewer | To be completed.
Retention schedule, data-rights owner, and processor list | To be completed.
Expected traffic, peak load, leads/orders, and media volume | To be completed.
Availability, RPO/RTO, and incident-support commitment | To be completed.
Deployment profile, region, budget, and dependency restrictions | To be completed.
Performance/accessibility targets and acceptance evidence | To be completed.
Launch dependencies, decision deadlines, and approval owners | To be completed.
Capability | Default | Required project decision
Public pages / CMS / forms / SEO / privacy / operations | Core | Named owner and acceptance tests.
Blog and resource capability | Core capability | Activate visible collections and editorial plan when content is ready.
Google Ads / Meta Ads | Configurable integration | Enable only with owned accounts, eligible business, approved conversion map, and consent/policy validation.
CRM synchronization | Growth | Provider, field ownership, backfill, webhook, error, and export policy.
Advanced analytics / replay | Growth | Business question, permission, collection limits, retention and reviewer. A/B capability is separately required in v1.1.
Commerce / subscription / booking / portal | Optional | Full lifecycle, data model, provider, support, and dedicated release gate.
Multilingual / search / newsletter / support channels | Optional | Locale/content/support owner and operating cost. Required contextual chatbot scope is governed by Chapter 37.
AI / PWA / community / marketplace / referral | Optional | Separate risk assessment, economic case, and failure/moderation workflow.
A/B experiments + UTM attribution | Required v1.1 capability | Owner workflow, stable assignment, analysis, source capture and form/chat/CRM tests; live activation governed by purpose and feasibility.
Visitor IP/location + contextual chatbot | Required v1.1 capability | Approve data purpose, scope, retention, protected projections, optional browser location task and processing terms.
Manual blog deletion + comprehensive audit | Core invariant | No automatic source/trash deletion; attributed human workflow; durable protected audit and recovery evidence.
ID | Requirement | Acceptance evidence
WEB-01 | Every important customer journey has working responsive pages and states. | End-to-end tests plus owner walkthrough.
WEB-02 | Published content, navigation, metadata, and share images render correctly. | Route crawl and template review.
WEB-03 | Performance budgets and field monitoring are implemented. | Build/lab/load reports and configured RUM.
WEB-04 | Accessibility target is evaluated on public and essential admin workflows. | Automated and manual evaluation report.
CMS-01 | Owner edits pages, blocks, menus, media, forms, and SEO without code. | Independent owner tasks completed.
CMS-02 | Draft/review/preview/schedule/publish/rollback is complete. | State-transition and cache refresh tests.
CMS-03 | Published revisions and forms retain version history. | Migration/rollback and submission-revision tests.
CMS-04 | Unapproved code/embeds cannot execute through the builder. | Injection and permission tests.
SEO-01 | Route-level index/canonical/sitemap/redirect policy exists. | Crawl, status, and metadata evidence.
SEO-02 | Content claims, sources, ownership, and update process are verified. | Editorial approval register.
SEO-03 | Old URLs and essential content survive migration intentionally. | URL map and production redirect checks.
ADS-01 | Business owns accounts and approves conversion goals. | Account/access register and goal map.
ADS-02 | Google conversion source/counting/deduplication is validated. | Account diagnostics and test conversion report.
ADS-03 | Meta browser/server contract is validated against current APIs. | Authenticated documentation/version record and diagnostics.
ADS-04 | Qualified-lead/sale feedback reflects real CRM/business state. | Lifecycle test and reconciliation evidence.
EVT-20 | Versioned event dictionary, SDK, validation, and field allowlists exist. | Contract tests and rejected-PII test payloads.
EVT-21 | Pricing, download, form, and page-view triggers mean what reports claim. | Browser tests across relevant devices.
EVT-22 | Business conversions originate from committed backend state. | Crash/retry/idempotency test.
PRIV-01 | Consent defaults, purpose choices, withdrawal, and queue suppression work. | Network/storage tests for each consent state.
PRIV-02 | No prohibited PII enters analytics, replay, URLs, or unrestricted logs. | Synthetic privacy audit and payload review.
PRIV-03 | Retention, rights requests, processor propagation, and backup reconciliation exist. | Deletion/export/restore exercise.
API-01 | Versioned REST contract and generated clients match implementation. | Contract validation in CI.
API-02 | Optional GraphQL/SOAP have the same domain rules and access protection. | Module-specific authorization and schema tests.
DB-01 | Keys, scoped uniqueness, constraints, migrations, and transactions protect data. | Real database integration tests.
DB-02 | Outbox and destination ledger survive retries and outages. | Worker restart, duplicate, and replay tests.
SEC-01 | Privileged MFA, secure sessions, scoped roles, and recovery are complete. | Authentication and access-control assessment.
SEC-02 | Uploads, content, requests, secrets, and dependencies are hardened. | Security review and vulnerability triage.
OPS-01 | Native/Compose profile runs from clean documented setup. | Second-developer bootstrap test.
OPS-02 | Deployment, rollback, observability, and incident alerts work. | Staging release and alert exercise.
OPS-03 | Backup restoration meets the approved recovery objectives. | Measured restore report.
OPS-04 | Accounts, source, data export, and provider exit paths belong to the business. | Handover and portability verification.
QA-01 | All critical/selected workflows have evidence and honest status. | Signed release register with exceptions.
Relationship | Integrity rule
Site → pages → revisions → block instances | Every child belongs to the same scoped site; live revision belongs to its page.
Post → author/reviewer → topics → related pages | References are valid and publishable; public queries do not reveal drafts.
Asset → derivatives → content usages | Access classification follows the original; in-use deletion is controlled.
Form → form revision → submission → lead | The exact submitted schema remains available; lead PII is protected.
Lead → activities → lifecycle events | Actual stage transitions create distinct audited events; note edits do not repeat conversions.
Business transaction → outbox → destination deliveries | One committed fact can fan out; each destination has its own eligibility and delivery state.
Consent/policy → eligible identity/touch → conversion mapping | Purpose and evidence remain traceable; unknown or withdrawn linkage is not fabricated.
Order → lines → payments/refunds → entitlements | Historical money snapshots remain immutable; verified adjustments change current entitlement under policy.
Field | Required information
Provider and purpose | Exact service, enabled modules, approved data purposes, and business owner.
Ownership and access | Business account owner, delegated users, recovery methods, MFA status, and permission review date.
Data and credentials | Field map, sensitivity, region/processor terms, secret-manager reference, and credential rotation owner.
Technical contract | API version, scopes, webhook/signature rules, quotas, retry policy, and deprecation review date.
Financial control | Billing owner, pricing basis, budget, usage alerts, renewal, and cancellation terms.
Exit | Export formats, data deletion, replacement adapter, migration steps, and last portability test.
Term | Meaning in this standard
CMS | The content-management interface used to maintain pages, articles, media, forms, and approved settings.
Headless / decoupled | Content/business services are accessed through interfaces rather than being inseparable from one presentation system.
SSR / pre-rendering / caching | Different ways to produce and reuse page output; choose by route freshness, privacy, and performance needs.
API / REST / GraphQL / SOAP | Interface mechanisms; REST is the default, while GraphQL and SOAP are selected only for justified needs.
GTM / dataLayer | Tag distribution/configuration and the browser event interface; neither is the authoritative business database.
GA4 | Google's analytics product; one reporting destination with its own processing rules and limitations.
Pixel / CAPI | Browser and server event connections for Meta; neither guarantees complete identity or attribution.
CMP / consent mode | Choice-management tooling and provider-specific consent behavior; not substitutes for an approved privacy policy.
PII / pseudonymous | Identifying personal data / data linked to an indirect identifier. Pseudonymous does not automatically mean anonymous.
Attribution | A rule-based interpretation of which touchpoints receive credit, distinct from the fact that a business event occurred.
Outbox / idempotency | Durable pending effects linked to a transaction / safe repetition without duplicating the same intended business action.
CRM | Customer and lead-management system containing authorized business records and lifecycle history.
Core Web Vitals | Field-oriented page-experience metrics; useful thresholds do not guarantee ranking or advertising results.
RBAC / object authorization | Role-based permissions / checks that a user may access the particular record or resource.
RPO / RTO / SLO | Acceptable data-loss window / recovery-time objective / measurable service objective approved for the project.
CI/CD / IaC | Automated validation and delivery / versioned infrastructure definitions.
Feature flag / adapter | Controlled module activation / a replaceable boundary for an external service.
Source of truth | The system whose record is authoritative for a specific fact, such as a paid order or published revision.
Event | Trigger and authority | Allowed context / key restriction
acquisition_touch_captured | Collector accepts eligible sanitized acquisition. | touch reference and campaign mapping; not a raw query string.
attribution_linked | Backend links an accepted lead to permitted evidence. | lead/touch references internally; no copied contact data.
experiment_assigned | Trusted resolver assigns an eligible unit. | experiment/version/variant and unit scope; not proof of exposure.
experiment_exposure | The agreed experience/render trigger is satisfied. | Same assignment reference and trigger version; deduplicate as declared.
experiment_paused | Authorized operator or approved safety rule pauses enrollment/delivery. | actor/rule, reason, version and affected scope; audit stream.
experiment_promoted | Approved result becomes a CMS publication. | decision and published revision references; audit/outbox.
chat_open | Visitor deliberately opens the chat interface. | launcher/page ID; optional behavioral event only when permitted.
chat_started | Backend accepts the first meaningful conversation interaction. | conversation/bot version internally; not a qualified lead.
chat_message_accepted | Chat backend durably accepts a message. | sender type, message ID and delivery status; text stays in conversation store.
chat_capture_started | Visitor begins explicit structured contact capture. | capture schema/version; no partial values in analytics.
chat_capture_confirmed | Visitor explicitly submits/accepts captured fields. | protected capture evidence; generate_lead still requires business commit.
chat_handoff_requested | Visitor or approved rule asks for an agent. | routing/service category and request ID.
chat_agent_joined | Authorized human agent joins the conversation. | agent reference and joined_at internally; separate from request.
chat_resource_selected | Visitor chooses a suggested public resource. | asset/version and action ID; actual download uses Chapter 14 events.
chat_resolved | Authorized actor or approved workflow marks resolution. | reason code, actor type and status; not proof of customer satisfaction.
location_request_started | Visitor activates a stated location task. | purpose/control ID; no coordinate in behavioral payload.
location_permission_result | The browser/app request resolves to a known state. | granted/denied/unavailable/etc.; restricted or permissioned diagnostics.
location_task_resolved | Backend produces an approved service-area result. | coarse service-area reference; exact location stays protected.
context_link_revoked | Rights/consent/session policy revokes context linkage. | policy and reference IDs; stop future eligible use.
content_trashed / restored | Authorized manual state transition commits. | actor, reason, version and operation; mandatory audit.
content_purge_requested / approved | Authorized human creates or approves a manifest. | requester/approver, scope hash, reason and expiry.
content_purge_executed | Approved execution reaches a recorded phase/result. | initiator, approver, executor, manifest and actual outcome.
sensitive_context_accessed | Authorized raw-IP/location/transcript reveal or export. | actor/purpose/evidence reference; do not duplicate revealed values.
audit_export_verified | Independent batch manifest/checkpoint verification succeeds or fails. | batch/sequence/digest status; integrity alert on failure.
Data class | Starting policy | Access / external transfer
Blog source posts and revisions, including trash | No automatic deletion. Retain until authorized manual source-content removal or lawful exception. | Editorial access; only published content is public. No automatic purge by analytics retention jobs.
Raw visitor IP | Avoid persistence when transient processing is sufficient; otherwise propose a short security window such as 7–30 days, subject to approved obligations. | Security/approved adapter only; no generic analytics dimension or chatbot prompt.
Precise browser coordinates | Prefer in-memory task processing and immediate discard; exceptional persistence needs a stated task-specific expiry. | Protected location service; return a derived service-area result where possible.
Approximate location and behavioral events | Default to the approved Chapter 17 short-window event schedule; coarse data only. | Purpose-controlled analytics with minimum cohort protections.
UTM touches and click identifiers | Declared attribution lookback and destination eligibility/expiry; not unlimited history. | Restricted identifier store; only eligible destination-specific transmission.
Chat transcripts and attachments | Proposed 30–90 days for support triage unless case-specific obligations justify another period. | Assigned support staff and approved processing; no session replay or ad payloads.
Accepted leads and verified business records | Business/legal schedule under Chapter 17, independent from transcript expiry. | Authorized CRM/support/business integrations.
Experiment assignments/results | Keep identifiable enrollment only through the approved analysis/maturation window; retain justified aggregate decisions longer. | Restricted analysis; published decisions do not expose personal units.
Minimal critical audit evidence | Security/legal owner approves a justified period, for example 12 months where appropriate; hold rules explicit. | Append-oriented restricted storage with independent verification; no ordinary admin erasure.
Backups and exports | Approved rolling expiry; short-lived export links; restore-time deletion reconciliation. | Encrypted, restricted and audited; backup expiry does not delete live retained blogs.
ID | Scenario | Required evidence / expected result
AB-01 | Owner creates and previews A/B variants. | Pinned approved revisions, accessible preview, hypothesis/metric and approval recorded.
AB-02 | Repeat navigation/reload and permitted return visits. | Stable unit/variant; no fresh randomization on every render.
AB-03 | No optional measurement permission or configuration outage. | Usable documented control; no hidden-page flicker or forbidden tracking.
AB-04 | SSR, hydration, CDN and multiple app instances. | Correct bounded variant cache; no cross-visitor content/Set-Cookie leak.
AB-05 | Assignment versus actual exposure. | Separate counts, symmetric trigger and defined exposure deduplication.
AB-06 | A/A and sample-ratio mismatch fixture. | Instrumentation validated; invalid sample prevents a winner decision.
AB-07 | Low traffic and premature analysis. | Insufficient-data/inconclusive state; no unsupported success badge.
AB-08 | Multiple arms, segments and late outcomes. | Predeclared analysis/correction/maturation; exploratory results labeled.
AB-09 | Conflicting tests or changed treatment. | Mutual exclusion or approved overlap; new version/cohort for material change.
AB-10 | Kill switch and promotion/rollback. | Audited action; control/previous revision available; verified publication refresh.
AB-11 | Split-URL search and ad review. | No cloaking; appropriate canonical and temporary redirect behavior.
UTM-01 | Builder generates campaign links. | Approved vocabulary, stable ID, no PII and no unresolved macros.
UTM-02 | Tagged landing through all redirect types. | Eligible genuine identifiers reach final route without corruption.
UTM-03 | Navigation, download, form and chatbot. | Same eligible acquisition evidence carried by validated references.
UTM-04 | Direct return and a later different campaign. | First/last/non-direct models behave as documented; earlier lead snapshot unchanged.
UTM-05 | Bad encoding, long values, duplicate/PII-bearing parameters. | Sanitized or rejected input; no raw query leakage in edge/app logs.
UTM-06 | Hidden-field or reference tampering. | Cannot attach another session/site's context or authorize a business action.
UTM-07 | Cross-domain transfer and third-party returns. | Allowed destination/linker only; no long-lived identity/PII leakage.
UTM-08 | Consent refused/withdrawn, blocked storage and unknown source. | No retrospective replay; reporting distinguishes unknown from direct.
UTM-09 | Campaign rename and spend/CRM joins. | Stable keys, source provenance, currency/timezone and model explained.
GEO-01 | Spoofed forwarding headers and origin bypass. | Trusted topology enforced; forged client IP never accepted as authority.
GEO-02 | IPv4, IPv6, multiple/variable proxy hops. | Correct source/config attribution; malformed addresses rejected.
GEO-03 | VPN, shared network and missing GeoIP result. | Approximate/unknown labels; no named-person or street-address claim.
GEO-04 | Ordinary page load and chat open. | No automatic precise-location prompt.
GEO-05 | Explicit location action with permission granted. | Stated purpose; source, timestamp and accuracy recorded in protected context.
GEO-06 | Denial, timeout, unavailable, cancellation and late callback. | Useful manual fallback; no repeated prompt or late unwanted persistence.
GEO-07 | Out-of-range, stale, mocked and (0,0) positions. | Correct validation; simulated location never treated as identity proof.
GEO-08 | Iframe restrictions and in-app browsers. | Safe behavior; no permission bypass; first-party control when possible.
GEO-09 | Analyst/bot/ads payload inspection. | No raw IP or exact coordinates in generic events/prompts/replay.
GEO-10 | Location retention and privileged reveal/export. | Expiry/disposal works; actor/purpose/access logged; permission boundaries enforced.
CHAT-01 | Chat start with analytics and ads refused. | Functional conversation without optional tracking or reconstructed history.
CHAT-02 | Current page, locale and eligible acquisition. | Purpose-specific shared context; correct provenance and no duplicate tracker.
CHAT-03 | Structured fields and AI-extracted contact candidate. | Explicit visitor submission/confirmation; no lead from unsent keystrokes.
CHAT-04 | Lead submit retry, reconnect and browser/server fan-out. | One accepted business event per idempotent action and correct destination identity.
CHAT-05 | Location request from chat. | Same permission/task flow; denial uses manual selector, not fake GPS.
CHAT-06 | Guessed conversation ID, forged context and tenant switch. | Authorization denies cross-visitor/site access.
CHAT-07 | Email typed for an existing customer. | No account/history reveal without appropriate ownership verification.
CHAT-08 | Prompt injection and forbidden tool call. | Model cannot expose secrets, purge blogs, disable logs or bypass authorization.
CHAT-09 | Trashed/unpublished source or changed price. | Retrieval excludes stale/private source; price uses authoritative current rule.
CHAT-10 | AI outage, handoff and closed support hours. | Approved fallback; honest delivery states; main forms remain usable.
CHAT-11 | Transcript/replay/log/export and rights request. | Masking, scoped access, approved deletion/retention and processor handling.
BLOG-01 | Advance time beyond one year; run cleanup jobs. | Active, draft and trashed source posts/revisions persist.
BLOG-02 | CMS plugin, bucket lifecycle and database TTL audit. | No automatic source-content or trash purge policy remains enabled.
BLOG-03 | Manual trash with required reason. | Exact actor/object/version/time and reason committed with audit/outbox.
BLOG-04 | Unauthorized user, bot or integration deletion attempt. | Denied through UI/API and restricted database path; failure evidence recorded.
BLOG-05 | Restore a trashed post with slug/schema conflict. | Safe conflict handling; restore to unpublished state; no silent overwrite.
BLOG-06 | Delete author/category or shared media. | No cascade deletion of posts or other recoverable source content.
BLOG-07 | Purge requester also acts as approver. | Default two-person rule enforced; no silent exception.
BLOG-08 | Changed manifest, expired approval, hold or revoked permission. | Execution denied/requires renewed approval; exact scope remains bound.
BLOG-09 | Interrupted approved purge and retry. | Idempotent phase ledger; original human and execution service identifiable.
BLOG-10 | Public routes, feeds, search and chatbot after removal. | Correct status/index/cache/retrieval invalidation; private revisions inaccessible.
BLOG-11 | Staff account deactivated after deletion. | Minimal justified attribution survives; no cascade loss of audit evidence.
BLOG-12 | Restore an older backup after lawful purge. | Independent deletion ledger prevents unauthorized resurrection.
AUD-01 | Each critical administrative/business action. | Coverage register maps emitter, atomic audit entry, export and test.
AUD-02 | Domain transaction fails or authorization denies. | No false committed action; separate failure/attempt evidence survives rollback.
AUD-03 | Local durable audit write unavailable. | Protected mutation fails rather than completing invisibly.
AUD-04 | Remote export unavailable, local evidence healthy. | Durable queued export, lag alert and reconciliation; no false success report.
AUD-05 | Ordinary admin attempts audit update/delete. | Denied; independent evidence and security alert exist.
AUD-06 | Audit batch altered, removed or reordered. | Manifest/checkpoint verification detects the relevant integrity/gap failure.
AUD-07 | Log producer receives secrets/PII/control characters. | Redaction and injection protection; no sensitive full-body dumps.
AUD-08 | Sensitive reveal, search and export. | Scope/purpose checks, safe evidence, expiry and access audit.
AUD-09 | Retention expiry and legal hold. | Data-class policy honored; no blog purge; disposal evidence minimized.
AUD-10 | Clock skew, worker execution and staff deactivation. | UTC/recording times and initiator/approver/executor remain distinguishable.
INT-01 | Native and Docker deployment. | Equivalent context, deletion and audit invariants; clean bootstrap.
INT-02 | Feature disabled or provider disconnected. | No orphan tags/jobs/privileges; safe fallback and retained required records.
INT-03 | Production migration and content import. | No reset/demo overwrite; preserved revisions and explicit rollback plan.
INT-04 | Owner runs the integrated demonstration. | Can operate the new capabilities without routine developer intervention.
INT-05 | Full release evidence review. | Actual pass/fail/blocked status, residual risks and accountable sign-off.

Entity | Required keys/fields | Integrity rule
workflow_versions | site_id, workflow_id, version, schema, state, approved_by, hash | Published version immutable; scoped unique version.
workflow_runs | site_id, run_id, workflow_version, subject_ref, trigger_event_id, status | Enrollment key unique under declared re-entry scope.
node_executions | site_id, run_id, node_id, iteration, operation_id, status | One logical execution key; retries retain identity.
workflow_timers | run/node reference, due_at, lease_until, attempts | Durable scheduling; expired leases recover safely.
message_intents | site, recipient_ref, purpose, template_version, operation_id | Dispatch uses current eligibility, not only enrollment-time consent.
provider_receipts | account, operation_id, provider_id, status, received_at | Scope and supported unique receipt prevent duplicate processing.
audience_actions | segment_version, destination, subject_ref, action, policy_version | Suppression/removal can override pending additions.
report_sync_runs | account, source_grain, cursor, window, extracted_at, status | Backfill and recent-period restatement are resumable.
release_manifests | site, manifest_id, item/version hashes, approval, status | Exact approved revision set; expected versions prevent substitution.
Field/data class | Business service | Analytics | Advertising adapter | AI response context
Safe page/action IDs | Allowed for the task | Purpose-permitted | Approved mapping only | Relevant approved page/topic only.
UTM/campaign IDs | Eligible attribution reference | Sanitized permitted mapping | Supported mapping | Usually excluded; no need for raw click identifiers.
Contact details | Explicit capture, protected access | No direct identifying values | Only eligible supported matching transformation | Exclude unless the specific task requires a minimized projection.
Raw IP/user-agent | Restricted approved operational/matching use | No generic raw-IP profile | Only under current contract and approved purpose | Excluded.
Browser coordinates | Explicit location task, limited retention | No precise coordinates | Excluded by default | Derived service-area answer rather than coordinates.
Chat/form text | Protected case store | No raw text | No transcript/answers | Necessary conversation turns after filtering and scope checks.
Payment secrets/tokens | Only approved payment/secret systems | Never | Never | Never.
Required field | To be completed before build/activation
Identity and business | Site/legal entity, accountable owner, business model, real offers and operating locations.
Audience and service | Countries, languages, age/sensitivity considerations, accessibility needs, support hours and capacity.
Outcomes | Primary conversion, qualification rules, value/currency, source of truth and correction policy.
Content | Sitemap/templates, author/reviewer ownership, approved claims/media and manual preservation rules.
Platform scope | Universal/growth/conditional modules, build-versus-buy decisions, deployment profile and tenant model.
Data | Field/purpose map, region/processors, notice/consent, retention/holds/rights and sensitive-route exclusions.
Accounts | Domain/DNS/hosting/repository/CMS/CRM/analytics/ads/mail/payment owners and secure recovery references.
Targets | Traffic/peak workload, performance/accessibility, availability, RPO/RTO, latency and freshness.
Delivery | Team responsibilities, budget scenarios, provider approval dependencies, milestones and acceptance owner.
Topic | Current edition treatment | Required implementation evidence
Google offline/enhanced-lead ingestion | Data Manager guidance and legacy upload restrictions checked; use the currently supported access model. | Account/API authorization, payload fixtures, real diagnostics and correction rules.
Google campaign/bidding/AI features | Capabilities described with account/type-dependent controls; no permanent numerical limits promised. | Current UI/API contract, supported configuration and controlled experiment.
Meta objectives/policies/audiences/native leads | Several detailed pages were login-gated or failed public retrieval. References are explicit verification links. | Authorized current official documentation, asset/scopes, policy review and real end-to-end tests.
Meta Pixel/CAPI | Inherited deduplication invariant retained; exact parameters/windows are release checks. | Same-action browser/server diagnostics, timestamps, policy and retry tests.
Search features and structured data | Current support differs from the full Schema.org vocabulary; no rich-result guarantee. | Live supported-type register, visible-data consistency and crawl/render evidence.
Merchant/retail feeds | Current Merchant API migration/product specification consulted. | Destination account eligibility, field validation and item diagnostics.
Email | Gmail sender rules and RFC one-click mechanism consulted. | Actual sender volume/type rules, received headers, unsubscribe and suppression tests.
Privacy/sector/children | Framework plus inherited India phased-commencement caveat; no blanket worldwide legal opinion. | Jurisdiction and sector decision signed by qualified reviewer with implementation tests.
Runtime/framework/database | Architectural roles retained; versions must be supported and pinned at build time. | Compatibility/security/support matrix and upgrade/restore tests.
Hosting/cost | Categories and formulas, not permanent vendor prices. | Dated quotations, load assumptions, quotas, billing alerts and support staffing.
Term | Meaning in this standard
Capability versus activation | The system may implement a feature while leaving real collection/sending/spending disabled pending approval.
Draft acknowledgment | The server committed a version; it is not a claim the content is public.
Collection schema | Approved definition of content fields and relationships, not arbitrary executable application logic.
Release manifest | A pinned, approved set of content/configuration revisions with impact and rollback evidence.
Workflow version/run | An immutable automation definition and one subject's execution of that version.
Dispatch gate | Final current eligibility check before an external effect, distinct from earlier enrollment.
Suppression | A controlled rule preventing future eligible processing/sends; not always the same as deleting a business record.
Score contribution | An explainable input/rule effect in a derived score, not verified qualification.
Segment/audience | An internal rule-defined population versus a provider-specific targeting/matching product.
Remote uncertainty | A request may have reached a provider but its effect is not yet confirmed; blind retry may be unsafe.
Feed snapshot | A versioned destination projection of authoritative catalogue data with validation and status.
Metric grain | What one row/count represents, such as event, session, lead, order or campaign-day.
Attribution | A declared rule assigning credit to observed eligible touches; not necessarily causation.
Incrementality | Estimated change caused by an intervention under an appropriate comparison design.
SPF/DKIM/DMARC | Email sender authentication/signature/alignment policy mechanisms that require configuration and verification.
GPC | A browser/user opt-out signal honored where the applicable legal/policy profile requires it.
PITR | Point-in-time recovery using an appropriate backup/log strategy, validated through restoration.
SBOM | Software bill of materials supporting dependency, license and vulnerability management.
Conditional sector pack | A site-specific business/legal/data/operational specification for an activated specialized industry workflow.
Evidence packet | The actual observations, versions, tests, approvals and residual risks supporting a release decision.

Clarification | Governing locations | Reason
Unified inbox delivery versus channel activation | 42, 48, 76, 78 | The comprehensive platform capability is required; each external channel remains explicitly activated.
Raw IP sharing boundary | 36, 41, 52, 69, 78 | Generic analytics/model sharing is prohibited; a narrowly approved destination adapter is different.
Primary/secondary custom-goal exception | 12, 51, 67 | Effective bidding can include a secondary action in a custom goal.
Google ingestion and credentials | 16, 51, 69 | Capture current Data Manager paths and the legacy authorization transition; avoid old tutorial assumptions.
Search AI activation/reporting | 11, 49, 66 | Include actual account settings and newly documented aggregate reports.
Metadata stripping versus provenance | 08, 44, 54, 66 | Preserve destination-required AI/rights metadata while removing unnecessary private data.
SDK automatic enrichment | 13, 16, 52, 69 | Validate the transmitted serialized payload, not only the pre-SDK object.
Browser lifecycle | 06, 15, 34, 72 | Restored/prerendered/hidden pages require explicit measurement and privacy behavior.
Authoritative final acceptance | 30, 64, 78, Appendix P | Separate specification, implementation, activation and evidence; no blank requirement passes.

Route family | Index policy | Cache policy | Measurement/access rule
/programmes/{slug} | Canonical published route eligible for discovery | Public site/locale/revision cache | Consented behavior only; price validated in business actions.
/blog/{slug} | Canonical published article; remove derived discovery on manual unpublish | Public revision cache | Source and trash never age-expire.
/campaigns/{slug} | Explicit SEO decision for each campaign/variant | Bounded approved variant key | Preserve eligible acquisition; no private identity in key.
/search | Deliberate crawl/index policy | Bounded query/result policy | No raw sensitive query in analytics.
/preview/{token} | Private preview, no public indexing | No shared public cache | Expiring authorization; no production ad tags.
/account/* | Private | Protected response/session policy | Object authorization; replay and ad tags off by default.
/downloads/{resource} | Resource-specific access and file-header policy | Public or signed private delivery | Download click/authorization is not proof of reading.
Value | Meaning
Observed | The approved system directly recorded the event or field at its stated boundary.
Declared | The visitor or operator supplied it; verification may still be required.
Inferred | A method estimated it; store method/version and uncertainty.
Verified | Defined evidence satisfied a specific verification test; not universal identity certainty.
Unknown | No reliable value is available.
Not collected | The system intentionally did not collect it under the policy.
Expired/revoked | An earlier reference can no longer authorize current use.
Provider attributed | The provider assigned credit under its own model; not an additional business sale.
Decision | Required evidence/owner | Initial status
Business and audience | Legal entity, offers, markets, age considerations and accountable owner | To be completed for the site.
Scope | All 120 capability entries reviewed; industry modules and exclusions explained | To be completed for the site.
Content and claims | Real assets, rights, pricing, proof, reviewers and publication policy | To be completed for the site.
Manual deletion | Manual-only default; independent purge approval and any single-owner exception | To be confirmed; no automatic purge.
Data and privacy | Field purposes, legal/provider review, access, retention and rights workflow | To be completed for the site.
Architecture | Supported versions, deployment profile, integrations, capacity and exit plan | To be completed for the site.
Marketing and ads | Goals, campaign/creative/offer map, accounts, spend authority and effective settings | To be completed before live activation.
Operations | Support staffing, alerts, recovery objectives, costs and maintenance ownership | To be completed before launch.
